Verifies feasibility, requirements clarity, design judgment, codebase consistency, and (when the spec has UI content) UI-pattern alignment in one pass. Writes findings to the output_path from the prompt. ([neko-neko/belt](https://github.com/neko-neko/belt/blob/HEAD/plugins/belt/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec axis of a two-axis code review. Checks a diff against what was asked for, reading an ISA (claims, anti-claims, decisions) first when the repo or task has one, else the issue or spec it is given. Read-only. Dispatched by the code-review skill alongside standards-reviewer; also usable alone as a fresh-context second look on a finished slice. ([omar-the-junior/skills](https://github.com/omar-the-junior/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer for OpenPlush spec, plan, data-model, contract, and task-list changes. Hunts for contradictions across documents, decisions without a ledger entry, and designs that break the crate DAG or the constitution. Use on any PR that touches specs/ or CONSTITUTION.md. ([openplush/openplush](https://github.com/openplush/openplush/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Independent spec document reviewer. Use this agent to evaluate and improve IP design specifications (spec.md) before presenting to the user for confirmation. Catches ambiguities, missing details, and internal inconsistencies. ([Paradicat/demo](https://github.com/Paradicat/demo/blob/HEAD/agent_pack/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a staged diff against the originating ticket/plan/spec. Reports missing requirements, scope creep, and wrong implementations. Read-only. Runs in parallel with @standards-reviewer. Use when full-cycle pipeline reaches the review step. ([paschalidi/opencode-config](https://github.com/paschalidi/opencode-config/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a built screen against SPEC.md and DESIGN_SYSTEM.md for spec compliance. Use at gate close when screens or components were built. ([PCSchmidt/SITREP](https://github.com/PCSchmidt/SITREP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Independent spec reviewer. Receives only the spec (plus repo and read access to production) and a role — architecture, facts, or production — and attacks it from that angle. Read-only. Use on class A/B specs before the build starts. ([rafae2k/rafoflow](https://github.com/rafae2k/rafoflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/shapeup/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only adversarial reviewer. Use before merging significant changes — verifies diffs against the roadmap's locked decisions, the adopted external standards, and repo conventions, and hunts for drift between schemas, generated files, code, and the book. ([RafaelAugustScherer/domainbook](https://github.com/RafaelAugustScherer/domainbook/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews one mstack spec independently and adversarially before any code is written. Returns APPROVED or CHANGES_REQUESTED. Never edits the spec and never reviews a spec it wrote. ([romerma/mstack](https://github.com/romerma/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Relecteur adversarial d'une PR de spec (spec, tests de contrat, scénarios cachés) ou de cadrage (produit, domaine, roadmap, ADR). À utiliser avant la lecture humaine, jamais par l'agent qui a rédigé la spec dans le même contexte. ([s4mf3nn/skyline](https://github.com/s4mf3nn/skyline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Use when a written interphase spec and its Steps need one fresh-eyes review before hand-off. ([SatanshuMishra/interphase](https://github.com/SatanshuMishra/interphase/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Use this agent for verifying that an implementation matches its task spec exactly — nothing missing, nothing extra. Dispatched by the build skill after the implementer completes. Read-only — never modifies code. ([SentioLabs/pi-nexus](https://github.com/SentioLabs/pi-nexus/blob/HEAD/packages/pi-arc/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: 仕様レビュー専門家。/spec 完了後や spec/* ブランチのPR前に PROACTIVELY 使用。EARS記法・AC品質・網羅性をチェック。 ([Sn005/recommend-scp](https://github.com/Sn005/recommend-scp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: 要件定義ループのレビュー役。harness/spec.md の下書きを読み、あいまいな点・抜け・矛盾・機械で確かめられない受け入れ条件を指摘する。spec-interview スキルから呼ぶ。書き込みはしない。 ([Takapon0407/docker-laravel](https://github.com/Takapon0407/docker-laravel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a spec in specs/ for completeness and testability before it is accepted. Use after drafting or editing a spec.md, before asking the human to approve it. ([terryong31/ai-shield](https://github.com/terryong31/ai-shield/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec compliance reviewer. Checks if implemented code matches the original specification and design document. First stage of the two-stage subagent review process. Invoked automatically after each implementation task in subagent-driven workflows. ([theopengroup/rune-harness](https://github.com/theopengroup/rune-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Use this agent when specification review is requested, after running /check-spec or /generate-spec commands when quality checks are needed, or when users say 'review spec', 'check specification', 'review design', or 'check design doc'. Reviews abstract specs under .sdd/specification/ and the technical design draft at .sdd/task/{ticket-number}/design-draft.md (a v4.x persistent .sdd/specification/*_design.md is also accepted) for CONSTITUTION.md compliance, checking for ambiguous descriptions, missing sections, SysML validity, and PRD/spec/design traceability plus spec/adr decision consistency… ([ToshikiImagawa/ai-sdd-workflow-dev](https://github.com/ToshikiImagawa/ai-sdd-workflow-dev/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-workflow/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Tool-neutral role definition. The `.claude/agents/` and `.cursor/agents/` files are thin adapters that add tool-specific frontmatter and point here. This role enforces **maker ≠ checker** (SPEC §12): whoever implemented the slice must not run this review of it. ([transon-org/transon-authoring-skill](https://github.com/transon-org/transon-authoring-skill/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Adversarial pre-merge review of a diff against docs/SPEC.md (maker ≠ checker). Verifies claimed requirement IDs, traceability, gates, and engine-grounded claims. Read-only on product code. ([transon-org/transon-authoring-skill](https://github.com/transon-org/transon-authoring-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Adversarial pre-merge review of a diff against docs/SPEC.md (maker ≠ checker). Read-only on product code. ([transon-org/transon-authoring-skill](https://github.com/transon-org/transon-authoring-skill/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a design spec for one named aspect, or reads it in full for blocking defects, and writes a findings file. ([ViktorGozhiy/ship-happens](https://github.com/ViktorGozhiy/ship-happens/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Review SDD feature specifications for Kotlin/Spring/ArrowKt projects for completeness, consistency, testability, and Clean Architecture compliance. ([wakita181009/clean-architecture-kotlin](https://github.com/wakita181009/clean-architecture-kotlin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Validate implementation against task acceptance criteria. Checks each criterion individually and reports which are met. Used by astra orchestrator evaluator loop. ([weardo/astra](https://github.com/weardo/astra/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: PR作成前に、実装セッションとは別のコンテキストで「origin/developとの差分」と「担当issue・spec.mdの受け入れ条件」を突き合わせる読み取り専用のレビュアー。受け入れ条件の充足・テストの有無・担当範囲外の変更・正確性の欠陥・層の責務違反だけを重大度付きで報告する。 ([wknd-studio/brand-closed-ec](https://github.com/wknd-studio/brand-closed-ec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: The second review axis: checks the PR diff against the original task (task file / issue / brief). Three questions — what was asked and is missing, what is present and was not asked (scope creep), what looks done but is done wrong. Does not look at code correctness (that is code-reviewer) and does not look at security (that is security-reviewer). Dispatched on EVERY PR that has an original task. Output in English. ([yaremenko-maksym/CheekyCheeseIT_CRM](https://github.com/yaremenko-maksym/CheekyCheeseIT_CRM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Review the complete change (proposal -&gt; design -&gt; specs -&gt; tasks), assess verifiability, and output a structured review report. Also handles escalation mode — root-cause analysis when a task fails 3 fix attempts. ([Yuchenhui/harness-spec](https://github.com/Yuchenhui/harness-spec/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Review specification consistency. Detect over-engineering and unnecessary defensive code. ([YunosukeYoshino/harness](https://github.com/YunosukeYoshino/harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks an implementation against its spec and flags every acceptance criterion that is not fully met. Use after /implement to validate before merging. Input: the spec file path and the implementation file paths to audit. Output: a structured verdict table (PASS / FAIL / PARTIAL / UNVERIFIABLE) with a specific code citation for every criterion. ([yuvarajd85/ai-ml-learnings-exemplars](https://github.com/yuvarajd85/ai-ml-learnings-exemplars/blob/HEAD/claude-projects/ticketflow-Demo/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: First-pass spec reviewer (Uber "first-pass PRD" style). Scores a normalized spec across fixed dimensions, classifies its risk, and returns a launch-readiness verdict with a single "start here" fix, prioritized Critical/Optimization action items, and write-ready replacement text. Read-only; runs before the human spec gate. ([zd87pl/cc-loop-boilerplate](https://github.com/zd87pl/cc-loop-boilerplate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks behaviour, schemas, and delivery claims against the numbered ContextOS specification. Use before closing a requirement or claiming a phase gate. ([zentelligence/contextos](https://github.com/zentelligence/contextos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: 在 OpenSpec change 的需求文件(proposal/design/tasks/specs)寫完後 呼叫,在進入實作前審查。檢查需求完整性、邊界情況、安全與權限需求、AC 與測試 任務的雙向對應、與既有規格的一致性,並對文件中對既有程式碼行為的具體事實 宣稱做有限度的原始碼核對。不評論程式碼風格或實作細節,那是 strict-reviewer 的工作。 ([a620906209/ticket-hub](https://github.com/a620906209/ticket-hub/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks a change against memoirist's README promises, specs (API_CONTRACT, DATABASE_SCHEMA, DECISIONS, USER_FLOWS) and rules (security, api, backend, frontend). Use after finishing a sprint task or before committing, e.g. "have spec-reviewer check this task". ([aarrroon/memoirist](https://github.com/aarrroon/memoirist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Stage 1 of the SDD two-stage review gate. Runs after every implementer run and BEFORE code-reviewer. Verifies the implementation matches the spec exactly — nothing more, nothing less — by reading actual code, not trusting the implementer's report. Does NOT evaluate code quality. Read-only. Adapted from obra/superpowers SDD. ([Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023](https://github.com/Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 收片用的「Spec 轴」只读审查:输入任务书(或契约)+ diff,只报三类——要求了没做 / 没要求却做了 / 做了但看起来不对,每条引任务书原句。不审 bug 不审风格(那是 /code-review 的轴),不改任何文件。 ([AbyssCN/claude-lead-harness](https://github.com/AbyssCN/claude-lead-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Review a feature's spec.md, plan.md, tasks.md or fix.md in specs/, or a requirements.md in docs/requirements/, against the constitution and higher-level documents. Use after every creation or edit of these documents. ([alannguyen2003/ShopManagement](https://github.com/alannguyen2003/ShopManagement/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a spec file (docs/specs/&lt;feat&gt;/spec.md) for ambiguity, missing acceptance criteria, hidden assumptions, and untestable claims. Acts as the clarify-gate before /feature-plan begins. ([aleburrascano/claude-discipline-template](https://github.com/aleburrascano/claude-discipline-template/blob/HEAD/plugins/bootstrap/content/core/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Requirements analyst that reviews docs/project_spec.md for completeness, clarity, and scope discipline. Use proactively before any building starts, and after major additions to the spec. ([AleCaballeroM/AIBuilder](https://github.com/AleCaballeroM/AIBuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a diff against the spec it was meant to implement. Reports what is missing, what was built that nobody asked for, and what looks implemented but does not match what the criterion says. Dispatched by self-review; not for general code review. ([AlpayC/claude-skill-set](https://github.com/AlpayC/claude-skill-set/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only conformance review of implemented work against the feature spec, plan, and constitution. Use before opening a PR, or on request against an existing PR branch. Never modifies files. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only review specialist. Use to review a change for correctness, security, performance, convention adherence, and (for UI) design-spec conformance. Returns prioritized, evidence-backed findings with file:line references. Never edits code — it judges, it does not fix. ([AnandSGit/Healer](https://github.com/AnandSGit/Healer/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a diff in a fresh context against the backlog task's acceptance criteria and the PRD, then reports gaps. Use before marking a task done or merging a PR. ([andrelnunes/agent-framework-template](https://github.com/andrelnunes/agent-framework-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Fresh independent, read-only review of a TASK or cumulative implementation against written AC, scenarios, scope, and design. Runs before quality review. ([andrewr303/ultimate-claude-code](https://github.com/andrewr303/ultimate-claude-code/blob/HEAD/plugins/ultimate-sdd/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a code change against its module SPEC, the DESIGN sections it cites and the determinism rules, with fresh context. - **spec-reviewer**: Use after implementing or changing a TicketFlow feature to verify the code matches its approved spec under specs/. Checks every acceptance criterion is met, flags BRD requirements that aren't traced, and catches behavior that silently diverges from the spec. Invoke proactively at the end of /implement, and always as part of /verify. ([anjumrizwi/TicketFlow](https://github.com/anjumrizwi/TicketFlow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: You are reviewing a SuperSpec specification document (`spec.md`) for quality and completeness. Your job: verify every functional requirement is testable and unambiguous, every user story is independently demonstrable with clear Given/When/Then structure, and the spec is free of lingering clarification requests and vague language. ([anupam20sep/IRA](https://github.com/anupam20sep/IRA/blob/HEAD/content/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Use when reviewing a SuperSpec spec.md for quality — every FR testable, every user story independently demonstrable, no lingering ambiguity. ([anupam20sep/IRA](https://github.com/anupam20sep/IRA/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a diff against its ticket's acceptance criteria and maps each criterion to concrete evidence. Read-only. Use after implementing a ticket, before writing its review brief. ([appleapplegaming/islam-history](https://github.com/appleapplegaming/islam-history/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Independent, read-only reviewer. Given a spec folder under specs/, checks the implementation and its tests against every numbered acceptance criterion and reports gaps. - **spec-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks a change against its frozen spec, across backend AND frontend: every targeted BR is implemented and has a named test, no behaviour exists that the spec doesn't describe, and the frontend calls match the backend contract. Complements code-reviewer (which checks code style/architecture). Trigger: review against spec, spec compliance, did I cover the rules, contract check, before merge. ([ArunDoesCode/ERP-diecast](https://github.com/ArunDoesCode/ERP-diecast/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: The independent read-only review AGENTS.md requires before a work item moves to PASS. Give it the commit range, the work item ids and the spec sections they implement. ([asapacsin/iOS_Automotive_Voice_Agent](https://github.com/asapacsin/iOS_Automotive_Voice_Agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Use to find blocking gaps in specifications. ([Asylcreek/home-manager](https://github.com/Asylcreek/home-manager/blob/HEAD/dots/agents/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only auditor for spec.md and plan.md. Runs EARS-conformance + plan-tag audits, returns a compact verdict + findings list. Non-interactive — the orchestrator owns the human review loop. Keeps full file contents out of the orchestrator context. ([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: docs/work/&lt;이슈&gt;/spec.md를 구현 전에 검토한다. 빈틈, 모호함, PRD·확정 결정(D-xx)과의 충돌, API·이벤트 계약 문제, 검증할 수 없는 수용 기준을 찾는다. /spec 단계에서 호출한다. ([atlas-han/gatehouse](https://github.com/atlas-han/gatehouse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Relit les changements de la branche courante par rapport à docs/SPEC.md. À utiliser après l'implémentation d'une fonctionnalité, avant le commit final ou la pull request. ([basilevanha/rayon](https://github.com/basilevanha/rayon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: One perspective of the spec review, started by /spec (decision 0021). - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a diff against the EchoCare spec. - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews implementation for spec compliance. Verifies the implementer built exactly what was requested — nothing missing, nothing extra. Does not trust the implementer's self-report. ([brightidea/do-work](https://github.com/brightidea/do-work/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Fresh-context, unbiased judge for spec docs - fresh-eyes review, failure-mode coverage, completeness, consistency. Never judges a plan; dispatch plan-reviewer for that. Dispatch for to-spec and self-review spec gates. Input: spec path + question. ([brunogsa/unix-utils](https://github.com/brunogsa/unix-utils/blob/HEAD/configs/ai-docs/claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews specifications for completeness and testability. Independent validation. ([BryceEWatson/bryce-labs-toolkit](https://github.com/BryceEWatson/bryce-labs-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-workflow/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a finished task's changes against the specs in docs/ and the PS judge checklist. Use after implementing any task, before ticking it in TASKS.md. ([buildwithpriince/suraksha-saathi](https://github.com/buildwithpriince/suraksha-saathi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Stage-one review. Read-only. Checks an implementer's diff strictly against the task's acceptance criteria. Runs before quality-reviewer. ([Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates](https://github.com/Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness-base/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a plan or implementation against the target repo's canonical requirement specs — openspec/specs/ when the repo uses OpenSpec, otherwise the kit structure (docs/specs/ for canonical specs, docs/features/&lt;change-id&gt;/ for change specs, docs/design/ for architecture deliverables). Points out scope creep (an item in the change outside the requirements) and gaps (a requirement the change should deliver but does not). Use whenever a planned change implements or extends an existing capability, when the user asks for "spec review", "capability review", "check the requirements" or "check agains… ([chacal88/aidakit](https://github.com/chacal88/aidakit/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a plan or implementation against the project's canonical requirement specs (openspec/specs/ or docs/specs/) and returns one verdict with a per-requirement coverage table, flagging scope creep (work no requirement asks for) and gaps (required scenarios not delivered). Use when a change implements or extends an existing capability, someone asks "spec review" or "check against the requirements", or before approving a plan/diff that cites a spec. ([chacal88/devkit](https://github.com/chacal88/devkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/spec-reviewer.md))

- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews specs against code (and optionally a PRD) for accuracy. Two modes: post-build (PRD + specs + code) or reverse (specs + code only). ([codethread/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/codethread/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/agents/spec-reviewer.md))

- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks a Hashi diff against docs/spec acceptance criteria and invariants. Cannot edit files. ([cwhde/hashi](https://github.com/cwhde/hashi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review all spec documents for completeness, consistency, and gaps. ([dannySubsense/agent-rig](https://github.com/dannySubsense/agent-rig/blob/HEAD/archive/spec-orchestration-03132026/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Post-green gate agent for the parrot cycle. Judges the final diff against the task brief - missing features and unrequested features both fail. Read-only. ([devakchow/parrot](https://github.com/devakchow/parrot/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Judges whether one written specification may enter development — free of self-contradiction, current with the code, complete from itself alone, and testable — against the closed admission register. ([Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin](https://github.com/Devscribed/Devscribed.Admin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews PulseGate code changes for conformance with CLAUDE.md invariants, accepted ADRs and the spec documents. - **spec-reviewer**: Use as stage 1 of review, after an implementer reports and before code review — answers one question, "does this diff do exactly what was asked, nothing missing and nothing extra?". Read-only. Distrusts the implementer's report, reads the brief and the diff, and maps every requirement to file:line or MISSING / EXTRA / AMBIGUOUS. Verdict SPEC_OK or SPEC_GAPS. ([donjmilligan/wall3_agent](https://github.com/donjmilligan/wall3_agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))

- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a diff or file set against its spec sources — linked issues, their workspaces, and domain spec docs. ([vpr1995/prudhvi-software-factory](https://github.com/vpr1995/prudhvi-software-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))

- **spec-reviewer**: 规格统一审查员（v11，取代逐文档 gate-reviewer）。三份规格文档齐备并通过 spec-lint.sh 后由 /spec-run 或 /spec-tasks 派发，一次派发完成跨文档实质审查，输出 PASS/FAIL。lite.md 不派发本审查（self-check + spec-lint 覆盖）。 ([wynn5a/agent-projects](https://github.com/wynn5a/agent-projects/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Независимый проверяющий. Сверяет спецификацию, код и фактический прогон проверок и пишет отчёт о расхождениях в docs/review/. Ничего не исправляет. Вызывать перед вехой В4, перед мёржем крупных PR и перед защитой. На вход — область проверки (весь проект, список REQ или номер PR). - **spec-reviewer**: auth/AI/DB/roleplay 등 위험 변경의 스펙 준수 독립 리뷰어. 구현과 다른 시각에서 diff를 스펙(screen/openapi/data-model)과 대조해 누락·위반·edge case 미처리를 찾는다. 읽기 전용 — 코드를 고치지 않는다. ([yyj9529/nextmadi](https://github.com/yyj9529/nextmadi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))

- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a change against its page or journey spec in docs/ (structure, states, words, scope). Use after building or changing a page. Read-only. ([zohaf/lineup-bridge](https://github.com/zohaf/lineup-bridge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review specification documents for consistency, completeness, and testability. Use after discovery, before planning. ([Zxela/claude-create](https://github.com/Zxela/claude-create/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer-a**: Requirements and acceptance-coverage reviewer for the specification review gate. Fresh, read-only context; returns structured findings only. ([aharada54914/sdd-forge](https://github.com/aharada54914/sdd-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-review-loop/agents/spec-reviewer-a.md))
- **spec-reviewer-agent**: Use this agent to review a pinned diff against the Spec axis (missing requirements, scope creep, false implementation, and S4 test evidence against applicable acceptance points) of code-review-protocol. ([winter6205/arthurpower](https://github.com/winter6205/arthurpower/blob/HEAD/arthurpower/agents/spec-reviewer-agent.md) · ★2)
- **spec-reviewer-agent**: You are a meticulous reviewer checking whether an implementation matches its specification. ([badrusiddique/enggenie-skill](https://github.com/badrusiddique/enggenie-skill/blob/HEAD/skills/dev-implement/agents/spec-reviewer-agent.md))
- **spec-reviewer-agent**: You are a senior product engineer reviewing a specification for completeness, clarity, and testability before it goes to engineering. ([badrusiddique/enggenie-skill](https://github.com/badrusiddique/enggenie-skill/blob/HEAD/skills/pm-refine/agents/spec-reviewer-agent.md))
- **spec-reviewer-b**: Independent specification risk and ambiguity reviewer for the specification review gate. Fresh, read-only context; receives only counts-and-IDs summary from reviewer A. - **spec-reviewer-conventions**: Review an engineering spec for adherence to repo conventions and prior decisions. Reads CLAUDE.md, the project wiki (if any), and greps the codebase for established patterns. Flags premature abstractions, contradictions with prior decisions, and unneeded backwards-compat shims. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-conventions.md))
- **spec-reviewer-correctness**: Review an engineering spec for correctness — does the proposed implementation actually solve the problem the brief states? Verifies every claim about current code by reading the actual files. Surfaces internal contradictions, references to nonexistent symbols, unsatisfied acceptance criteria, and stale anchors. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-correctness.md))
- **spec-reviewer-edge-cases**: Review an engineering spec for edge cases and failure modes. Probes empty/null/zero-length inputs, concurrency, external-system failures, limits, runtime-precondition violations, and debuggable-tripwire requirements. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-edge-cases.md))
- **spec-reviewer-executor**: PROACTIVELY 仕様書を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性・未定義要素・制約違反を検出して、誰が実装しても同じ結果になる決定論的仕様への書き換えを発行する仕様検証専門エージェント。 以下のいずれかに該当する場合に使用する。 - 仕様書（要件定義書 / 基本設計書 / 内部設計書 / API 仕様書 等）を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性を網羅的に検出したい - 仕様内の未定義要素（暗黙の前提・欠落条件・例外未定義）を抽出したい - 仕様が「誰が書いても同じ実装になる」レベルの決定論性を満たすか判定したい - 制約違反（ネットワーク障害・並行性・分散システム前提）を検出したい - 失敗定義（失敗種別・検出方法・システム挙動）の網羅性を検証したい - 仕様を決定論的状態（曖昧性 0 / 単一実装 / 計測可能値）に書き換えたい - 保証境界（保証対象 / 非保証対象 / 条件）を明示化したい - 仕様の最終判定（決定論的 / テスト可能 / 実装可能）を取得したい 使用しないケース：仕様の作成・抽出そのもの（→ spec-items-clarifier スキル）／コード実装の検証（→ code-review-executor）／設計判断のみで仕様文書が存在しない場合（→ system-basic-design / clean-architecture スキル）／仕様書の単純な構文・誤字修正のみ… ([YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData](https://github.com/YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer-executor.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer-ko**: implementer-ko 산출 코드가 acceptance-criteria.md 와 spec.md 에 정확히 부합하는지 검증하는 specops-ko Phase B Evaluator. 코드 품질이 아닌 "스펙 준수" 만 평가. ([andyko18/devFSD](https://github.com/andyko18/devFSD/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-ko.md))

- **spec-reviewer-prompt**: Premier temps de la relecture. Un agent `kwa-reviewer` NEUF (jamais l'implémenteur) : `subagent_type: "kwa-reviewer"`, `model: "sonnet"`. - **spec-sampler**: 規格覆蓋度取樣 agent。讀一組指定的原始碼檔，逐一識別業務邏輯區塊（SCB）並比對行為規格的覆蓋度與正確性，回傳結構化取樣報告。由 spec-check --sample skill 分組後平行派發，不主動觸發。唯讀，不改任何檔。 ([KerryHuang/plugharbor](https://github.com/KerryHuang/plugharbor/blob/HEAD/plugins/lifecycle/agents/spec-sampler.md) · ★1)

In update mode you emit a lightweight delta (ADDED / MODIFIED / REMOVED hunks) AND apply it in place with Edit — only the sections this PR's diff touches, git is the archive, no free-hand rewrite of untouched content.
